## 添加追蹤規則 (網絡邏輯示意圖工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　a1ba549902d6754db9-53

**english**　Add Trace Rule

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 網絡邏輯示意圖工具箱
- 配置工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-trace-rule.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-trace-rule.html

**parameter**　condition_barriers

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- condition_barriers [[Name, Operator, Type, Value, Combine Using],...] （可選）
- 基于與網絡屬性的比較或對類別字符串的檢查，對圖徵設置可遍歷性障礙條件。 條件障礙根據網絡屬性或類別、操作符、類型及屬性值來定義。 例如，當圖徵的 Device Status 屬性等于特定值 Open 時，將停止追蹤。 當圖徵滿足此條件時，追蹤將停止。 值表列： Name—Filter by any network attribute defined in the system. Operator—Choose from a number of different operators. IS_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is equal to the value. DOES_NOT_EQUAL—The attribute is not equal to the value. IS_GREATER_THAN—The attribute is greater than the value. IS_GREATER_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. IS_LESS_THAN—The attribute is less than the value. IS_LESS_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. INCLUDES_THE_VALUES—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_THE_VALUES—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). INCLUDES_ANY—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_ANY—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Type—Choose a specific value or network attribute from the value that is specified with the name. SPECIFIC_VALUE—Filter by a specific value. NETWORK_ATTRIBUTE—Filter by a network attribute. Value—Provide a specific value for the input attribute type that would cause termination based on the operator value. Combine Using—Set this value if you have multiple attributes to add. You can combine them using an And or an Or condition. AND—Combine the condition barriers. OR—Use if either condition barrier is met.
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-trace-rule.html#derived-output

## 添加追蹤規則 (網絡邏輯示意圖工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　a1ba549902d6754db9-54

**english**　Add Trace Rule

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 網絡邏輯示意圖工具箱
- 配置工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-trace-rule.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-trace-rule.html

**parameter**　function_barriers

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- function_barriers [[Function, Attribute, Operator, Value, Use Local Values],...] （可選）
- 基于函數對圖徵設置可遍歷性障礙。 函數障礙可用于執行以下操作：限制追蹤距離起點的行程或設置停止追蹤的最大值。 例如，所經過的每條線的長度和為目前經過的總距離。 當經過的總長度達到指定值時，追蹤將停止。 值表列： Function—Choose from a number of different calculation functions. MIN—The minimum of the input values will be used. MAX—The maximum of the input values will be used. AVERAGE—The average of the input values will be used. COUNT—The number of features will be used. ADD—The sum of the values will be used. SUBTRACT—The difference between the values will be used. Subnetwork controllers and loops trace types do not support the subtract function. Attribute—Filter by any network attribute defined in the system. Operator—Choose from a number of different operators. IS_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is equal to the value. DOES_NOT_EQUAL—The attribute is not equal to the value. IS_GREATER_THAN—The attribute is greater than the value. IS_GREATER_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. IS_LESS_THAN—The attribute is less than the value. IS_LESS_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. INCLUDES_THE_VALUES—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_THE_VALUES—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). INCLUDES_ANY—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_ANY—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Value—Provide a specific value for the input attribute type that, if discovered, will cause the termination. Use Local Values—Calculate values in each direction as opposed to an overall global value. For example, a function barrier that is calculating the sum of Shape length in which the trace terminates if the value is greater than or equal to 4. In the global case, after you traverse two edges with a value of 2, you will have reached a shape length sum of 4, so the trace stops. If local values are used, the local values along each path change, and the trace continues.
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-trace-rule.html#derived-output

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- traversability_scope （可選）
- 指定將應用的可遍歷性類型。 可遍歷性范圍用于定義是否將在交匯點和/或邊處應用可遍歷性。 例如，如果定義了用于停止追蹤的條件障礙，其中 Device Status 等于 Open 并將遍歷范圍僅設置為邊，則即使追蹤遇到開路設備，追蹤也不會停止，因為 Device Status 僅適用于交匯點。 換言之，此參數會為追蹤指明是否要忽略交匯點和/或邊。 BOTH_JUNCTIONS_AND_EDGES—可遍歷性將同時應用于交匯點和邊。 這是默認設置。 JUNCTIONS_ONLY—可遍歷性將僅應用于交匯點。 EDGES_ONLY—可遍歷性將僅應用于邊。
- String

**values**　
- filter_barriers [[Name, Operator, Type, Value, Combine Using],...] （可選）
- 用于指定針對特定類別或網絡屬性的追蹤停止時間。 例如，針對生命周期狀態屬性等于特定值的圖徵，追蹤停止。 此參數用于根據系統中定義的網絡屬性值設置終止符。 值表列： Name—Filter by category or any network attribute defined in the system. Operator—Choose from a number of different operators. IS_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is equal to the value. DOES_NOT_EQUAL—The attribute is not equal to the value. IS_GREATER_THAN—The attribute is greater than the value. IS_GREATER_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. IS_LESS_THAN—The attribute is less than the value. IS_LESS_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. INCLUDES_THE_VALUES—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_THE_VALUES—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). INCLUDES_ANY—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_ANY—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Type—Choose a specific value or network attribute from the value that is specified with the name. SPECIFIC_VALUE—Filter by a specific value. NETWORK_ATTRIBUTE—Filter by a network attribute. Value—Provide a specific value of the input attribute type that would cause termination based on the operator value. Combine Using—Set this value if you have multiple attributes to add. You can combine them using an And or an Or condition. AND—Combine the condition barriers. OR—Use if either condition barrier is met.
- Value Table

INCLUDES_ANY—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_ANY—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Value—Filter by a specific value. Use Local Values—Boolean value to use local variables instead of a global variable.

- 將用于按 bitset 過濾的網絡屬性的名稱。 此參數僅適用于上游、下游和循環追蹤類型。 此參數可用于在追蹤過程中添加特殊邏輯，以便追蹤能夠更好地反映真實世界的場景。 例如，對于循環追蹤而言，Phases current 網絡屬性可以確定該循環是否為實際的電氣循環（循環各處均由同一相供電，例如 A 相），并且追蹤結果只返回實際的電氣循環。 上游追蹤的示例如下；如果追蹤配電網絡時指定 Phases current 網絡屬性，則追蹤結果將只包含在網絡屬性中指定的有效路徑，而不是所有路徑。

## 添加追蹤規則 (網絡邏輯示意圖工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　a1ba549902d6754db9-65

**english**　Add Trace Rule

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 網絡邏輯示意圖工具箱
- 配置工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-trace-rule.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-trace-rule.html

**parameter**　propagators

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- propagators [[Attribute, Substitution Attribute, Function, Operator, Value],...] （可選）
- 指定在傳統域網絡中的追蹤過程中，需要傳播的網絡屬性及其傳播方式。 傳播的類屬性表示子網控制器上已傳播至子網余下圖徵的關鍵值。 例如，在配電模型中，您可傳播相位值。 值表列： Attribute—Filter by any network attribute defined in the system. Substitution Attribute—Use a substituted value instead of bitset network attribute values. Substitutions are encoded based on the number of bits in the network attribute being propagated. A substitution is a mapping of each bit in phase to another bit. For example, for Phase AC, one substitution could map bit A to B, and bit C to null. In this example, the substitution for 1010 (Phase AC) is 0000-0010-0000-0000 (512). The substitution captures the mapping so you know that Phase A was mapped to B and Phase C was mapped to null, and not the other way around (that is, Phase A was not mapped to null and Phase C was not mapped to B). Function—Choose from a number of calculation functions. PROPAGATED_BITWISE_AND—Compare the values from one feature to the next. PROPAGATED_MIN—The minimum value will be used. PROPAGATED_MAX—The maximum value will be used. Operator—Choose from a number of operators. IS_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is equal to the value. DOES_NOT_EQUAL—The attribute is equal to the value. IS_GREATER_THAN—The attribute is greater than the value. IS_GREATER_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. IS_LESS_THAN—The attribute is less than the value. IS_LESS_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. INCLUDES_THE_VALUES—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_THE_VALUES—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). INCLUDES_ANY—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_ANY—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Value—Provide a specific value for the input attribute type that would cause termination based on the operator value.
- Value Table

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-trace-rule.html#derived-output

- 指定追蹤網絡中最短路徑的方向。 遍歷路徑的成本取決于 path_network_weight_name 參數值。 僅在針對追蹤網絡應用 SHORTEST_PATH 追蹤類型時，才會使用此參數。 NO_DIRECTION—最短路徑位于兩個起點之間，無論流向如何。 這是默認設置。 PATH_UPSTREAM—最短路徑方向將是兩個起點之間的上游方向。 PATH_DOWNSTREAM—最短路徑方向將是兩個起點之間的下游方向。

- path_network_weight_name （可選）
- 在使用路徑或最短路徑追蹤類型時，用于計算公共設施網絡或追蹤網絡最短路徑的網絡屬性。 運行最短路徑追蹤類型時，使用數字網絡屬性（例如形狀長度）計算最短路徑。 基于成本和距離的路徑都可以進行計算。 運行最短路徑追蹤時，需要此參數。
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-trace-rule.html#derived-output

## 添加追蹤規則 (網絡邏輯示意圖工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　a1ba549902d6754db9-74

**english**　Add Trace Rule

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 網絡邏輯示意圖工具箱
- 配置工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-trace-rule.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-trace-rule.html

**parameter**　output_conditions

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- output_conditions [[Name, Operator, Type, Value, Combine Using],...] （可選）
- 按網絡屬性或類別返回的圖徵類型。 例如，如果將追蹤配置為“過濾掉除水龍頭圖徵外的所有圖徵”，那么未分配水龍頭類別的追蹤圖徵均不會包含在結果中。 任何追蹤的圖徵都會返回到結果選擇集中。 如果要使用多個屬性，可使用 Combine Using 選項來定義 And 或 Or 條件。 值表列： Name—Filter by network attribute name. Operator—Filter by an operator. IS_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is equal to the value. DOES_NOT_EQUAL—The attribute is not equal to the value. IS_GREATER_THAN—The attribute is greater than the value. IS_GREATER_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. IS_LESS_THAN—The attribute is less than the value. IS_LESS_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. INCLUDES_THE_VALUES—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_THE_VALUES—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). INCLUDES_ANY—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_ANY—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Type—Filter by a specific type. SPECIFIC_VALUE—Filter by a specific value. NETWORK_ATTRIBUTE—Filter by a network attribute. Value—Filter by a specific value. Combine Using—Use this parameter to combine multiple conditions. AND—Combine the conditions. OR—Use if either condition is met.
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-trace-rule.html#derived-output

- 名稱
- 說明

- max_hops （可選）
- 路徑或電路中允許的邊之間的最大跳數（不包括起點）。 所有邊以及交匯點-交匯點連通性關聯在遍歷時均被評估為具有固定單位長度 1。 終端設備被評估為具有固定單位長度 2。 默認值為 100。 僅當 trace_type 參數設置為 PATH 或 CIRCUIT 時，此參數才有效。

**chapter**

**parameter**　infer_connectivity

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- infer_connectivity （可選）
- 指定路徑追蹤是否將使用連通性推斷來遍歷包含關聯并識別指定的起始位置與停止位置之間的路徑。 僅電信域網絡中的路徑追蹤支持此參數，可實現在未明確提供連通性信息時發現分段間的路徑。 有關詳細信息，請參閱推斷連通性。 INFER_CONNECTIVITY—當連通性不完整阻礙遍歷時，路徑追蹤將使用連通性推斷來遍歷包含關聯并識別指定的起始位置與停止位置之間的路徑。 DO_NOT_INFER_CONNECTIVITY—將不會使用連通性推斷來識別指定的起始位置與停止位置之間的路徑。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-trace-rule.html#derived-output

向給定網絡現有的 MyTemplate1 添加追蹤規則，以根據 ElectricDistribution 域中的中壓層內的子網追蹤生成邏輯示意圖。

**id**　a1ba549902d6754db9-81

**english**　Add Trace Rule

**text**　import arcpy

input_DiagramTemplate = "MyTemplate1"

arcpy.nd.AddTraceRule(input_Network, input_DiagramTemplate, "ACTIVE",
                      "SUBNETWORK", "ElectricDistribution", "Medium Voltage",
                      "Low Voltage Mesh")

## 添加追蹤規則 (網絡邏輯示意圖工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 網絡邏輯示意圖工具箱
- 配置工具集

**english**　Add Trace Rule

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-trace-rule.html

**id**　a1ba549902d6754db9-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-diagram/add-trace-rule.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Add Trace Rule，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入網絡 [in_utility_network]、輸入邏輯示意圖模板 [template_name]、活動 [is_active]、追蹤類型 [trace_type]、域網絡 （可選） [domain_network]、層 （可選） [tier]、目標層 （可選） [target_tier]、包括結構 （可選） [include_structures]、包括障礙圖徵 （可選） [include_barriers]、條件障礙 （可選） [condition_barriers]、函數障礙 （可選） [function_barriers]、應用可遍歷性至 （可選） [traversability_scope]、過濾器障礙 （可選） [filter_barriers]、過濾器函數障礙 （可選） [filter_function_barriers]、將過濾器應用于 （可選） [filter_scope]、按 bitset 網絡屬性過濾 （可選） [filter_bitset_network_attribute_name]、按最近過濾 （可選） [filter_nearest]、計數 （可選） [nearest_count]、成本網絡屬性 （可選） [nearest_cost_network_attribute]、最近類別 （可選） [nearest_categories]、最近資產組/類型 （可選） [nearest_assets]、傳播程序 （可選） [propagators]、描述 （可選） [description]、允許不確定的流 （可選） [allow_indeterminate_flow]、路徑方向 （可選） [path_direction]、最短路徑網絡屬性名稱 （可選） [path_network_weight_name]、使用追蹤配置 （可選） [use_trace_config]、追蹤配置名稱 （可選） [trace_config_name]、使用數字化方向 （可選） [use_digitized_direction]、輸出資產類型 （可選） [output_assettypes]、輸出條件 （可選） [output_conditions]、路徑數 （可選） [num_paths]、最大跳數 （可選） [max_hops]、推斷連通性 （可選） [infer_connectivity]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_utility_network", "label_zh": "輸入網絡", "label_en": "Input Network", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Utility Network; Trace Network", "description_en": "The utility network or trace network containing the diagram template that will be modified."}, {"parameter": "template_name", "label_zh": "輸入邏輯示意圖模板", "label_en": "Input Diagram Template", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the diagram template that will be modified."}, {"parameter": "is_active", "label_zh": "活動", "label_en": "Active", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the rule will be enabled when generating and updating diagrams based on the specified template. Checked—The added rule will become enabled during the generation and update of any diagrams based on the input template. This is the default. Unchecked—The added rule will not become enabled during the generation or update of any diagrams based on the input template."}, {"parameter": "trace_type", "label_zh": "追蹤類型", "label_en": "Trace Type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the type of trace that will be run to build the diagram content. Connected—A connected trace will be run from the utility network or trace network elements currently represented in the diagram when the rule starts and span outward along connected elements. This is the default. Subnetwork—A subnetwork trace will be run from the utility network elements currently represented in the diagram when the rule starts and span outward along connected elements to find sources or sinks from which it spans outward along the related subnetwork. Upstream—An upstream trace will be run from the utility network or trace network elements currently represented in the diagram when the rule starts to discover elements upstream. Downstream—A downstream trace will be run from the utility network or trace network elements currently represented in the diagram when the rule starts to discover elements downstream. Shortest path—A shortest path trace will be run from the utility network or trace network features currently specified as starting points in the diagram when the rule starts to discover features along the shortest path between those starting points. The cost of traversing the path is determined based on the network attribute set for the Shortest Path Network Attribute Name parameter value regardless of flow direction. Legacy: The Shortest path trace type is deprecated with ArcGIS Pro 3.7 and ArcGIS Enterprise 12.1. While it will continue to function and be an option until the next major release, we recommend that you use the Path trace type to return a shortest path. The Path trace type provides the same functionality to return a shortest path result while offering additional capabilities to find paths in your network. Circuit—A circuit trace will be run from the utility network elements currently represented in the diagram when the rule starts. The trace returns circuit definition and path information for both physical and virtual circuits. This trace type is supported for telecom domain networks only. Set the Traverse Circuit parameter to Traverse downstream or Traverse upstream to extend the circuit trace. Path—A path trace will be run to identify valid paths between the starting and stopping points used as input trace locations for the diagram generation."}, {"parameter": "domain_network", "label_zh": "域網絡 （可選）", "label_en": "Domain Network (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the domain network where the trace will be run for a utility network. This parameter is required when running the subnetwork, upstream, downstream, and circuit trace types."}, {"parameter": "tier", "label_zh": "層 （可選）", "label_en": "Tier (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the tier where the trace will start for a utility network. This parameter is optional when running the connected trace type; it is required when running the subnetwork, upstream, and downstream trace types."}, {"parameter": "target_tier", "label_zh": "目標層 （可選）", "label_en": "Target Tier (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the target tier to which the input tier will flow for a utility network. If this parameter is missing for upstream and downstream traces, those traces will stop when they reach the boundary of the starting subnetwork. This parameter can be used to allow these traces to continue either farther up or farther down the hierarchy."}, {"parameter": "include_structures", "label_zh": "包括結構 （可選）", "label_en": "Include structures (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether structure features and objects will be included in the trace results. Checked—Structure features and objects will be included in the trace results. Unchecked—Structure features and objects will not be included in the trace results. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "include_barriers", "label_zh": "包括障礙圖徵 （可選）", "label_en": "Include barriers features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the traversability barrier features will be included in the trace results. Traversability barriers are optional even if they have been preset in the subnetwork definition. This parameter does not apply to device features with terminals. Checked—Traversability barrier features will be included in the trace results. This is the default. Unchecked—Traversability barrier features will not be included in the trace results."}, {"parameter": "condition_barriers", "label_zh": "條件障礙 （可選）", "label_en": "Condition Barriers (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Sets a traversability barrier condition on features based on a comparison to a network attribute or check for a category string. A condition barrier uses a network attribute or network category, an operator and a type, and an attribute value. For example, stop a trace when a feature has the Device Status attribute equal to the specific value of Open. When a feature meets this condition, the trace stops. Value table columns: Name—Filter by any network attribute defined in the system. Operator—Choose from a number of different operators. Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Includes the values (bitwise AND equals the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). Does not include the values (bitwise AND does not equal the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). Includes any (bitwise AND equals True)—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). Does not include any (bitwise AND equals False)—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Type—Choose a specific value or network attribute from the value that is specified with the name. Specific value—Filter by a specific value. Network attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Value—Provide a specific value for the input attribute type that would cause termination based on the operator value. Combine Using—Set this value if you have multiple attributes to add. You can combine them using an And or an Or condition. And—Combine the condition barriers. Or—Use if either condition barrier is met."}, {"parameter": "function_barriers", "label_zh": "函數障礙 （可選）", "label_en": "Function Barriers (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Sets a traversability barrier on features based on a function. Function barriers can be used to do such things as restrict how far the trace travels from the starting point, or set a maximum value to stop a trace. For example, the length of each line traveled is added to the total distance traveled so far. When the total length traveled reaches the value specified, the trace stops. Value table columns: Function—Choose from a number of different calculation functions. Minimum—The minimum of the input values will be used. Maximum—The maximum of the input values will be used. Average—The average of the input values will be used. Count—The number of features will be used. Add—The sum of the values will be used. Subtract—The difference between the values will be used. Subnetwork controllers and loops trace types do not support the subtract function. Attribute—Filter by any network attribute defined in the system. Operator—Choose from a number of different operators. Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Includes the values (bitwise AND equals the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). Does not include the values (bitwise AND does not equal the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). Includes any (bitwise AND equals True)—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). Does not include any (bitwise AND equals False)—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Value—Provide a specific value for the input attribute type that, if discovered, will cause the termination. Use Local Values—Calculate values in each direction as opposed to an overall global value. For example, a function barrier that is calculating the sum of Shape length in which the trace terminates if the value is greater than or equal to 4. In the global case, after you traverse two edges with a value of 2, you will have reached a shape length sum of 4, so the trace stops. If local values are used, the local values along each path change, and the trace continues."}, {"parameter": "traversability_scope", "label_zh": "應用可遍歷性至 （可選）", "label_en": "Apply Traversability To (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the type of traversability that will be enforced. Traversability scope defines whether traversability will be enforced at junctions, edges, or both. For example, if a condition barrier is defined to stop the trace if Device Status is equal to Open and traversability scope is set to edges only, the trace will not stop—even if it encounters an open device—because Device Status is only applicable to junctions. In other words, this parameter indicates to the trace whether to ignore junctions, edges, or both. Both junctions and edges—Traversability will be applied to both junctions and edges. Junctions only—Traversability will be applied to junctions only. Edges only—Traversability will be applied to edges only. Both junctions and edges—Traversability will be applied to both junctions and edges. This is the default. Junctions only—Traversability will be applied to junctions only. Edges only—Traversability will be applied to edges only."}, {"parameter": "filter_barriers", "label_zh": "過濾器障礙 （可選）", "label_en": "Filter Barriers (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Specifies when the trace will stop for a specific category or network attribute. This parameter is used to set a terminator based on a value of a network attribute that is defined in the system. Value table columns: Name—Filter by category or any network attribute defined in the system. Operator—Choose from a number of different operators. Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Includes the values (bitwise AND equals the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). Does not include the values (bitwise AND does not equal the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). Includes any (bitwise AND equals True)—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). Does not include any (bitwise AND equals False)—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Type—Choose a specific value or network attribute from the value that is specified with the name. Specific value—Filter by a specific value. Network attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Value—Provide a specific value of the input attribute type that would cause termination based on the operator value. Combine Using—Set this value if you have multiple attributes to add. You can combine them using an And or an Or condition. And—Combine the condition barriers. Or—Use if either condition barrier is met."}, {"parameter": "filter_function_barriers", "label_zh": "過濾器函數障礙 （可選）", "label_en": "Filter Function Barriers (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Filters the results of the trace for a specific category. Subnetwork controllers and loops trace types do not support the subtract function. Attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Operator—Filter by an operator. Use Local Values—Boolean value to use local variables instead of a global variable."}, {"parameter": "filter_scope", "label_zh": "將過濾器應用于 （可選）", "label_en": "Apply Filter To (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies whether the filter for a specific category will be applied to junctions, edges, or both. For example, if a filter barrier is defined to stop the trace if Device Status is equal to Open and traversability scope is set to edges only, the trace will not stop—even if the trace encounters an open device—because Device Status is only applicable to junctions. In other words, this parameter indicates to the trace whether to ignore junctions, edges, or both. Both junctions and edges—The filter will be applied to both junctions and edges. This is the default. Junctions only—The filter will be applied to junctions only. Edges only—The filter will be applied to edges only. Both junctions and edges—The filter will be applied to both junctions and edges. This is the default. Junctions only—The filter will be applied to junctions only. Edges only—The filter will be applied to edges only."}, {"parameter": "filter_bitset_network_attribute_name", "label_zh": "按 bitset 網絡屬性過濾 （可選）", "label_en": "Filter by bitset network attribute (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the network attribute that will be used to filter by bitset. This parameter is only applicable to upstream, downstream, and loops trace types. This parameter can be used to add special logic during a trace so the trace more closely reflects real-world scenarios. For example, for a loops trace, the Phases current network attribute can determine if the loop is a true electrical loop (the same phase is energized all around the loop, that is, A) and return only real electrical loops for the trace results. An example for an upstream trace is when tracing an electric distribution network, specify a Phases current network attribute, and the trace results will only include valid paths that are specified in the network attribute, not all paths."}, {"parameter": "filter_nearest", "label_zh": "按最近過濾 （可選）", "label_en": "Filter by nearest (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the k-nearest neighbors algorithm will be used to return a number of features of a certain type within a given distance. You can provide a count and a cost as well as a collection of categories, an asset type, or both. Checked—The k-nearest neighbors algorithm will be used to return a number of features as specified in the Count, Cost Network Attribute, Nearest Categories, or Nearest Asset Groups/Types. Unchecked—The k-nearest neighbors algorithm will not be used to filter results. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "nearest_count", "label_zh": "計數 （可選）", "label_en": "Count (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The number of features that will be returned when Filter by nearest is checked."}, {"parameter": "nearest_cost_network_attribute", "label_zh": "成本網絡屬性 （可選）", "label_en": "Cost Network Attribute (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The numeric network attribute that will be used to calculate nearness, cost, or distance when Filter by nearest is checked—for example, shape length."}, {"parameter": "nearest_categories", "label_zh": "最近類別 （可選）", "label_en": "Nearest Categories (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The categories that will be returned when Filter by nearest is checked—for example, protective."}, {"parameter": "nearest_assets", "label_zh": "最近資產組/類型 （可選）", "label_en": "Nearest Asset Groups/Types (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The asset groups and asset types that will be returned when Filter by nearest is checked—for example, ElectricDistributionDevice/Transformer/Step Down."}, {"parameter": "propagators", "label_zh": "傳播程序 （可選）", "label_en": "Propagators (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "This parameter is only available through Python. Value table columns: Attribute—Filter by any network attribute defined in the system. Substitution Attribute—Use a substituted value instead of bitset network attribute values. Substitutions are encoded based on the number of bits in the network attribute being propagated. A substitution is a mapping of each bit in phase to another bit. For example, for Phase AC, one substitution could map bit A to B, and bit C to null. In this example, the substitution for 1010 (Phase AC) is 0000-0010-0000-0000 (512). The substitution captures the mapping so you know that Phase A was mapped to B and Phase C was mapped to null, and not the other way around (that is, Phase A was not mapped to null and Phase C was not mapped to B). Function—Choose from a number of calculation functions. Propagated bitwise AND—Compare the values from one feature to the next. Propagated minimum—The minimum value will be used. Propagated maximum—The maximum value will be used. Operator—Choose from a number of operators. Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Includes the values (bitwise AND equals the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). Does not include the values (bitwise AND does not equal the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). Includes any (bitwise AND equals True)—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). Does not include any (bitwise AND equals False)—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Value—Provide a specific value for the input attribute type that would cause termination based on the operator value."}, {"parameter": "description", "label_zh": "描述 （可選）", "label_en": "Description (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The description of the rule."}, {"parameter": "allow_indeterminate_flow", "label_zh": "允許不確定的流 （可選）", "label_en": "Allow indeterminate flow (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether trace network features that have indeterminate or uninitialized flow will be traced. This parameter is only honored when running an upstream or downstream trace type. Checked—Trace network features that have indeterminate or uninitialized flow direction in the trace will be included. Unchecked—Trace network features that have indeterminate or uninitialized flow direction will not be included. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "path_direction", "label_zh": "路徑方向 （可選）", "label_en": "Path Direction (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the direction of the shortest path for a trace network. The cost of traversing the path is determined by the Shortest Path Network Attribute Name parameter value. This parameter is only honored when applying a Shortest path trace type to a trace network. No direction—The shortest path will be between the two starting points regardless of the direction of flow. This is the default. Upstream path—The direction of the shortest path will be upstream between the two starting points. Downstream path—The direction of the shortest path will be downstream between the two starting points."}, {"parameter": "path_network_weight_name", "label_zh": "最短路徑網絡屬性名稱 （可選）", "label_en": "Shortest Path Network Attribute Name (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The network attribute that will be used to calculate the shortest path using the path or shortest path trace type for a utility network or trace network. When running a shortest path trace type, the shortest path is calculated using a numeric network attribute such as shape length. Cost- and distance-based paths can both be achieved. This parameter is required when running a shortest path trace."}, {"parameter": "use_trace_config", "label_zh": "使用追蹤配置 （可選）", "label_en": "Use trace configuration (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether an existing named trace configuration will be used to define the properties of the trace. This parameter is only enabled for Utility Network version 7 and later. Checked—An existing named trace configuration will be used to define the properties of the trace. Unchecked—An existing named trace configuration will not be used to define the properties of the trace. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "trace_config_name", "label_zh": "追蹤配置名稱 （可選）", "label_en": "Trace Configuration Name (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the existing named trace configuration that will be used to define the properties of the trace. This parameter is only active when the Use Trace Configuration parameter is checked."}, {"parameter": "use_digitized_direction", "label_zh": "使用數字化方向 （可選）", "label_en": "Use digitized direction (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether upstream and downstream trace operations will determine flow using the digitized direction of the line and the flow direction attribute. This parameter is only enabled for Utility Network version 7 and later when the Trace Type parameter is set to the Upstream or Downstream option. Note: This parameter requires ArcGIS Enterprise 11.3 or later when using an enterprise geodatabase. Checked—The trace operations will determine flow direction using the digitized direction of the line and the flow direction attribute. With this option, the Domain Network, Tier, and Target Tier parameters are disabled. Unchecked—Trace operations will determine flow direction based on the location of subnetwork controllers. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "output_assettypes", "label_zh": "輸出資產類型 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Asset Types (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Filters the output asset types to be included in the results—for example, only return overhead transformers."}, {"parameter": "output_conditions", "label_zh": "輸出條件 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Conditions (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The types of features that will be returned based on a network attribute or category. For example, in a trace configured to filter out everything but Tap features, any traced features that do not have the Tap category assigned to them are not included in the results. Any traced features that do are returned in the result selection set. Value table columns: Name—Filter by network attribute name. Operator—Filter by an operator. Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Includes the values (bitwise AND equals the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). Does not include the values (bitwise AND does not equal the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). Includes any (bitwise AND equals True)—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). Does not include any (bitwise AND equals False)—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Type—Filter by a specific type. Specific value—Filter by a specific value. Network attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Value—Filter by a specific value. Combine Using—Use this parameter to combine multiple conditions. And—Combine the conditions. Or—Use if either condition is met."}, {"parameter": "num_paths", "label_zh": "路徑數 （可選）", "label_en": "Num Paths (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The maximum number of paths that will be returned in the path or circuit trace. The default is 1. This parameter is only active when the Trace Type parameter is set to Path or Circuit."}, {"parameter": "max_hops", "label_zh": "最大跳數 （可選）", "label_en": "Max Hops (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The maximum number of hops between edges allowed in the path or circuit, excluding the starting point. All edges and junction-junction connectivity associations are evaluated as having a fixed unit length of 1 on traversal. Terminal devices are evaluated as having a fixed unit length of 2. The default is 100. This parameter is only active when the Trace Type parameter is set to Path or Circuit."}, {"parameter": "infer_connectivity", "label_zh": "推斷連通性 （可選）", "label_en": "Infer connectivity (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the path trace will use connectivity inference to traverse containment associations and identify a path between the specified starting and stopping location. This parameter is only supported with the path trace in telecom domain networks and enables paths to be discovered between sections when connectivity information is not explicitly available. For more information, see Infer Connectivity. Checked—The path trace will use connectivity inference to traverse containment associations and identify a path between the specified starting and stopping location when incomplete connectivity prevents traversal. Unchecked—Connectivity inference will not be used to identify a path between the specified starting and stopping location. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "traverse_circuit", "label_zh": "", "label_en": "Traverse Circuit (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies whether the trace will extend to the downstream or upstream circuits. This parameter is only enabled when running the Trace Type parameter is set to Circuit. Do not traverse—The trace will only identify the network elements that make up the circuit. This is the default. Traverse downstream—The trace will extend beyond the circuit to include the downstream circuits. Traverse upstream—The circuit trace will extend beyond the circuit to include the upstream circuits."}, {"parameter": "traverse_virtual_circuit", "label_zh": "", "label_en": "Traverse virtual circuit (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the trace will extend beyond the circuit to include only the physical circuits that are downstream or upstream or will extend to include both the physical and virtual circuits. Checked—The circuit trace will extent beyond the circuit to include both physical and virtual circuits that are downstream or upstream. Unchecked—The circuit trace will extend beyond the circuit to include the physical circuits that are downstream or upstream. This is the default."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.nd.AddTraceRule(in_utility_network, template_name, is_active, {trace_type}, {domain_network}, {tier}, {target_tier}, {include_structures}, {include_barriers}, {condition_barriers}, {function_barriers}, {traversability_scope}, {filter_barriers}, {filter_function_barriers}, {filter_scope}, {filter_bitset_network_attribute_name}, {filter_nearest}, {nearest_count}, {nearest_cost_network_attribute}, {nearest_categories}, {nearest_assets}, {propagators}, {description}, {allow_indeterminate_flow}, {path_direction}, {path_network_weight_name}, {use_trace_config}, {trace_config_name}, {use_digitized_direction}, {output_assettypes}, {output_conditions}, {num_paths}, {max_hops}, {infer_connectivity}, {traverse_circuit}, {traverse_virtual_circuit})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_utility_network、template_name、is_active、trace_type、domain_network、tier、target_tier、include_structures、include_barriers、condition_barriers、function_barriers、traversability_scope、filter_barriers、filter_function_barriers、filter_scope、filter_bitset_network_attribute_name、filter_nearest、nearest_count、nearest_cost_network_attribute、nearest_categories、nearest_assets、propagators、description、allow_indeterminate_flow、path_direction、path_network_weight_name、use_trace_config、trace_config_name、use_digitized_direction、output_assettypes、output_conditions、num_paths、max_hops、infer_connectivity、traverse_circuit、traverse_virtual_circuit。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 匹配控制點 (資料管理工具) · 匯總

**id**　a1c73a8b4783a394ca-1

**english**　Match Control Points

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 網格工具集
- 正射映射

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/match-control-points.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/match-control-points.html

**text**　針對其中一個重疊影像中的給定地面控制點和初始連接點創建匹配的連接點。
為了得到更精確的平差，正射映射區域網平差工作流通常涉及添加地面控制點。 在每個重疊影像中，通常有一個地面控制點與連接點相關聯。 如果一個地面控制點對應多個重疊影像，則為每個影像手動創建連接點是一個勞動密集型過程。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/match-control-points.html#summary

## 匹配控制點 (資料管理工具) · 用法

**id**　a1c73a8b4783a394ca-2

**english**　Match Control Points

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 網格工具集
- 正射映射

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/match-control-points.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/match-control-points.html

**text**　如果輸入控制點集不包含地面控制點，則工具將失敗。
如果輸入控制點集包含地面控制點，但不包含初始連接點，則工具將失敗。
如果未找到關聯的連接點，則輸入控制點集將保持不變，并將顯示一條警告消息，指示未找到新的連接點。
輸入控制點集可以為點列表的 JSON 字符串對象，如下所示。 輸入控制點集 <!--```json [ { "poinId": 、 "x": "y": "z": "xyAccuracy": "zAccuracy": "spatialReference":{}, // 默認為 WGS84 "imagePointSpatialReference": , // 默認為 ICS "imagePoints": [ {"imageId": "x": "y": }, … ] } ]

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/match-control-points.html#usage

**id**　a1c73a8b4783a394ca-7

**english**　Match Control Points

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/match-control-points.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/match-control-points.html

**parameter**　similarity

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 相似 （可選）
- 指定將用于匹配連接點的相似性級別。 低級相似性—兩個匹配點的相似性條件為低級。 此選項將生成最匹配的連接點對，但是某些匹配連接點對的錯誤誤差等級可能比較高。 中級相似性—此匹配點對的相似性等級為中級。 高級相似性—此匹配點對的相似性等級為高級。 此選項將生成數目最少的匹配連接點對，但是每個匹配連接點對的誤差等級可能比較低。
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/match-control-points.html#parameters

**id**　a1c73a8b4783a394ca-16

**english**　Match Control Points

**text**　import arcpy
import json
mdpath = "c:/omproject/dronecollection.gdb/droneimgs"
initpointset = [
    {
        "x": -117.21684675264804,
        "y": 34.052400694386705,
        "z": 123,
        "pointId": 1,
        "imagePoints": [
            {
                "imageID": 7,

## 匹配控制點 (資料管理工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 網格工具集
- 正射映射

**english**　Match Control Points

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/match-control-points.html

**id**　a1c73a8b4783a394ca-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/match-control-points.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Match Control Points，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入鑲嵌資料集 [in_mosaic_dataset]、輸入控制點 [in_control_points]、輸出控制點表 [out_control_points]、相似 （可選） [similarity]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/match-control-points.html#parameters

**english**　Match Control Points

**id**　a1c73a8b4783a394ca-schema

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/match-control-points.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/data-management/match-control-points.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_mosaic_dataset", "label_zh": "輸入鑲嵌資料集", "label_en": "Input Mosaic Dataset", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Mosaic Dataset; Mosaic Layer", "description_en": "The mosaic dataset that contains the source imagery from which the tie points will be created."}, {"parameter": "in_control_points", "label_zh": "輸入控制點", "label_en": "Input Control Points", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "File; Feature Class; Feature Layer; String", "description_en": "The input control point set that contains a list of ground control point features and at least one initial tie point for each ground control point."}, {"parameter": "out_control_points", "label_zh": "輸出控制點表", "label_en": "Output Control Point Table", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Class", "description_en": "The output control point features that contain ground control points."}, {"parameter": "similarity", "label_zh": "相似 （可選）", "label_en": "Similarity (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the similarity level that will be used for matching tie points. Low similarity—The similarity criteria for the two matching points will be low. This option will produce the most matching points, but some of the matches may have a higher level of error. Medium similarity—The similarity criteria for the matching points will be medium. High similarity—The similarity criteria for the matching points will be high. This option will produce the fewest matching points, but each match will have a lower level of error."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.management.MatchControlPoints(in_mosaic_dataset, in_control_points, out_control_points, {similarity})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_mosaic_dataset、in_control_points、out_control_points、similarity。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

**text**　雖然此錯誤可能會發生，但發生的頻率非常低，以至于我們尚且無法確定典型原因，也因此目前沒有可用的解決方案。
如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。
幫助信息包括下列內容：
消息 ID 以及錯誤或警告消息
工具的名稱
用于參數的值
相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。
